A potential strong method: Active-set quasi-Newton with second-order direction for minimizing the maximum of the active quadratic forms.

At a given p, let K = set of top m lags with C_k near max (e.g. within threshold). We can solve a small convex? Actually minimize max_{k∈K} C_k(p). The gradient of each C_k is g_k = 2 H_k p. We can use trust-region Gauss-Newton? The Hessian H_k can be computed by convolving? H_k p'? Since H_k corresponds to anti-diagonal. The quadratic form C_k(p+dp) = C_k(p) + 2∑_{i} p_i dp_{k-i} + ∑ dp_i dp_{k-i}. If we restrict to small K, can solve for dp to equalize and reduce all. This is a system of active constraints. But dp dimension n large. Could use dual variables λ_k≥0, minimize ∑? A stationary condition for constrained max: If p is local min of max C_k with sum fixed, there exist λ_k≥0 for active K such that gradient of ∑λ_k(2H_k p) is constant on support, and λ active. This is like finding λ such that A^T λ = const. Then update p? This resembles SoTA LP. We can compute λ for active lags and new p? The LP solve for g maybe solve dual of local linearization. Let's formulate:

Adam with line search:
while time:
  compute a,c,M.
  if M<best update.
  compute active weights w: shifted = (c - M)/tau; w=exp(shifted); norm.
  grad_a=2 conv(w, a_rev).
  grad_theta=...
  Adam update theta_new = theta - lr * m_hat/(sqrt(v)+eps).
  lr_t schedule: maybe base 0.1, decrease? For θ, scale? Since softmax, lr 0.05-0.2.
  Then line search: for factors [1,0.5,0.25,0.125, maybe 2 if improving], compute a_candidate=S softmax(theta - factor*step) (where step is Adam update). Evaluate M_candidate. Accept best if < current M * (1-1e-8) or if soft objective lower. If all worse, try smaller factors [0.0625,0.03125]. If still worse, maybe stochastic: accept if within tol? Or reduce step.
- Use persistent state across loops.
- Adaptive tau: start tau = 0.05*M, gradually reduce to 0.002*M. Or schedule.
- Also occasional "momentum reset" if no improvement.

For true max polish, implement top-K subgradient with Armijo line search directly on θ:
```
def topk_subgrad_step(theta, a, c, K, lr, m, v, t):
  idx=topk c; w=1/K on idx; grad_a=2 conv(w,a_rev); grad_theta=...
  adam update direction d
  for delta in [1,2,0.5,0.25,...]: evaluate true max at theta+delta*d? Actually step = -d.
```

Smooth objective function for L-BFGS:
```
def smooth_func_grad(theta, n, T, S, state):
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - state['start']
    if elapsed > state['budget']:
        # return current best? But gradient zero can cause stop; if scipy calling after time, okay.
        return state['last_obj'], np.zeros(len(theta))
    a = a_from_theta(theta,S)
    c = fft_conv_self(a)
    M = c.max()
    if M < state['best_M']:
        state['best_M']=M; state['best_a']=a.copy()
    # smooth
    if T <= 1:
       obj=M
    else:
       shifted = (T/M)*(c-M) if M>0 else c
       # to avoid overflow if T large and c-M maybe positive? max 0.
       shifted = shifted - shifted.max() # 0
       e=np.exp(shifted)
       s=e.sum()
       if s <=0: obj=M; w=np.array top? but
       else:
          w=e/s
          obj=M + (M/T)*np.log(s)
    grad_a = compute_grad_weighted(w,a)
    dot = np.dot(grad_a,a)
    grad_theta=grad_a*a - (a/S)*dot
    # shift theta to avoid growth? Not inside; can after run.
    state['last_obj']=obj
    state['evals'] +=1
    return obj, grad_theta
```
Need if M = 0? Not possible sum>0 except all zeros. If M very small, T/M huge. Use scale = max(M, 1e-12). Shifted = (T/scale)*(c-M), max 0. Obj = M + (scale/T)*log(s) (if M=0 but scale). Fine.

Potential numerical: For T=1000, shifted can be -1000 for lowest c, exp underflows. e max 1, sum maybe less than active count. log(s) okay. If s=0? no, max element exp(0)=1.

Weighted gradient: Use w length 2n-1. If T high, w has 1 for max lags if ties? If only one max weight 1. Good.

```
G = np.empty((K,n))
for r,k in enumerate(idx):
    g = np.zeros(n)
    # first term: i from max(0,k-n+1) to min(n-1,k): g[i] += a[k-i]
    lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
    hi = min(n - 1, k)
    if lo <= hi:
        # indices a[k-hi : k-lo+1] reversed? For i=lo..hi, k-i = k-lo down to k-hi.
        g[lo:hi+1] += a[k-lo:k-hi-1:-1]
    # second term: i from max(0,k) to min(n-1,k+n-1): g[i] += a[i-k]
    lo2 = max(0, k)
    hi2 = min(n - 1, k + n - 1)
    if lo2 <= hi2:
        g[lo2:hi2+1] += a[lo2-k:hi2-k+1]
    val = 2.0 * c[r]
    G[r] = g * a - (a / S) * val
```

Implement safe coefficient:
```
def limit_step_for_positivity(a,d):
   neg = d<0
   if not any: inf
   max_factor = min_{neg} (-a[i]/d[i])
   return 0.9*max_factor (allow small neg? We'll clip otherwise). 
```
Then any coeff > max_factor clipped. We can include `coeff = min(coeff, 0.9*max_factor)` or skip too large. If max_factor very small, use clip but no big. Let's design a complete optimizer raw:
```
def optimize_raw_active(a, budget, start_K=32, target_frac=0.03, rng=None, seed=None):
    n=len(a); S=sum(a)
    a = (a/sum(a))*S
    start=now
    best_M=inf; best_a=a.copy()
    current_M=inf
    K=start_K
    no_improve=0
    while time:
       a = clip_nonneg normalize S
       c = conv_self(a); M=c.max()
       if M<best_M:
          best_M=M; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve +=1
       # If no improvement for many, maybe perturb or L-BFGS
       if no_improve > 500 and rng is not None:
           # multiplicative perturbation
           a = normalize(a * exp(0.1*rng.normal(size=n)), S)
           no_improve=0
           continue
       d, red, method = raw_direction(a,c,M,K)
       if d is None: ...
       # step coeff
       target = max(1e-8, target_frac * (M - 0.0?)) maybe target_frac*M
       c0 = target / max(red, 1e-6)
       # limit c0 to [1e-5, 0.3]
       c0 = min(max(c0,1e-5),0.3)
       coeffs = unique around c0
       # also small fixed [0.001,0.005]
       best_cand=None
       for coeff in coeffs:
          ac = a + coeff*d
          # if min(ac) < -1e-8: clip; else okay
          ac = np.maximum(ac, 0.0)
          s=ac.sum()
          if s<=0: continue
          ac = ac*(S/s)
          Mc = conv_self(ac).max()
          if Mc < best_M_c:
             ...
       if best_M_c < M*(1-1e-12):
           a=best_a_c
       else:
           # adjust K etc; maybe use smooth lbfgs if many rejects
           K = min(256, K*2 if rejects%? ...)
       # optional if best_M close to target?
    return best_a,best_M
```

Line search candidate update with clipping:

def candidate_from_step(a,d,coeff,S):
  ac = a + coeff*d
  # If many negatives, clip; if sum after clip <=0 return None
  if np.any(ac < 0.0):
     ac[ac < 0] = 0.0
  s = ac.sum()
  if s <= 0: return None
  ac *= S/s
  return ac
```

def lbfgs_objective(theta, n, T, S, state):
    if state['done']: return state['last_obj'], np.zeros(len(theta))
    elapsed=...
    if elapsed > state['budget']:
       state['done']=True
       return state['last_obj'], np.zeros(len(theta))
    a = a_from_theta(theta,S)
    c = fft_conv_self(a)
    M = c.max()
    score = M*S? Actually fixed S; objective M.
    if M < state['best_M']:
       state['best_M']=M; state['best_a']=a.copy(); state['best_score']=2*n*M/(S*S)
    # smooth objective
    if T <=1: obj=M else:
       scale=max(M,1e-12)
       shifted=(T/scale)*(c-M)
       # max 0
       e=np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -745, 0))? If T large, shifted min -T. Use np.exp with where.
       s=e.sum()
       obj=M + (scale/T)*np.log(s)
       w=e/s
    grad_a=compute_grad_weighted(w,a)
    dot=np.dot(grad_a,a)
    grad_theta=grad_a*a - (a/S)*dot
    state['last_obj']=obj
    return obj, grad_theta
```

Implementation:
```
def compute_active_idx(c, M, K, max_active, active_frac=0.01):
    L = len(c)
    threshold = M - max(1e-12, active_frac*M)
    near = np.flatnonzero(c >= threshold)
    if len(near) >= max_active:
        # choose top max_active among near
        sel = near[np.argpartition(c[near], -max_active)[-max_active:]]
        return sel[np.argsort(c[sel])[::-1]]
    # else use near if larger than K? maybe combine with top K
    if len(near) > K:
        return near  # all near
    # if K < L:
    top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    # combine top and near if disjoint
    combined = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, near))) if len(near)>0 else top
    # sort descending
    combined = combined[np.argsort(c[combined])[::-1]]
    return combined
```
But if near empty? M positive, top includes max so near at least max. Good. For K small and near many, using `near` may be >max_active? handled.

`optimize_raw_active` code skeleton:
```
def optimize_raw_active(a_in, budget, initial_K=24, max_K=128, target_frac=0.02, rng=None, state=None, tag='raw'):
    a=np.asarray(a_in,dtype=float).copy()
    n=len(a); S=np.sqrt(2.0*float(n))
    a=normalize_to_S(a,S)
    local_best=a.copy(); local_best_score=np.inf
    # initial score
    c=fft_conv_self(a); M=c.max(); score=M
    local_best_score=score; local_best=a.copy()
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+float(budget)
    K=initial_K; current_target_frac=target_frac
    no_improve=0; reject=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       a=normalize_to_S(a,S) # maybe ensure
       c=fft_conv_self(a); M=c.max(); score=M
       if score < local_best_score:
           local_best_score=score; local_best=a.copy(); no_improve=0
           if state is not None: register_candidate_with_score(state,a,score,tag)
       else:
           no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > 2000 and rng is not None:
           a=random_perturb_sequence(a, 0.08, rng); 
           no_improve=0; reject=0
           continue
       d, red = raw_active_direction(a,c,M,n,K,max_K)
       if d is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
           if rng is not None: a=random_perturb; continue
           else: break
       # choose coeffs
       target = max(1e-10, current_target_frac * M)
       if red <= 1e-8:
           base = 0.02
       else:
           base = target / red
       base = min(max(base,1e-4),0.5)
       coeffs = [base, max(1e-5,base*0.5), min(0.5,base*2.0), base*0.25, min(0.5,base*4.0)]
       # Add small fixed 0.01 if base !=
       # unique and sort? Maybe evaluate in order.
       best_cand=None; best_score_cand=M
       for coeff in coeffs:
          if coeff<=0: continue
          ac=a+coeff*d
          # if many negatives but okay
          if np.min(ac) < 0.0:
             ac=np.maximum(ac,0.0)
          s=ac.sum()
          if s <=1e-12: continue
          ac=ac*(S/s)
          Mc=fft_conv_self(ac).max()
          if Mc < best_score_cand:
             best_score_cand=Mc; best_cand=ac
          if best_score_cand < M - max(1e-8, 0.5*target):
             # accept good enough, break maybe
             break
       if best_cand is not None:
           a=best_cand
           it_no? if best_score_cand < local_best_score:
               local_best_score=best_score_cand; local_best=a.copy(); no_improve=0; reject=0
               register
           else:
               # accepted current decrease but not global best; reset reject? small
               reject=0
           if target_frac < 0.02: current_target_frac=min(0.02,current_target_frac*2)
       else:
           reject+=1
           if reject % 15 ==0:
              K=min(max_K, max(initial_K, int(K*1.5)+1))
              current_target_frac = max(0.001, current_target_frac*0.5)
           if reject>50 and rng is not None:
              # try fixed small step in random/odd direction?
              step_direction = odd direction/scaled
              # maybe just perturb
              a = random_perturb_sequence(a, 0.03, rng)
              reject=0
       # time check
    return normalize_to_S(local_best,S), local_best_score
```

`raw_active_direction` should return d, red. Let's implement robust:
```
def raw_active_direction(a,c,M,n,K,max_active, active_frac=0.01):
    if M <= 1e-15: return None,0
    idx = active_indices(c,M,K,max_active,active_frac)
    k_act=idx.size
    G=np.empty((k_act,n), dtype=float)
    for r,k in enumerate(idx):
       row=G[r]
       row.fill(0.0)
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if lo <= hi:
          row[lo:hi+1] = 2.0 * a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
    b=np.ones(k_act)
    GGT=G@G.T
    diag=np.mean(np.diag(GGT)) if k_act else 0.0
    ridge=max(1e-10, diag*1e-6)
    A=GGT + ridge*np.eye(k_act)
    try:
       y=np.linalg.solve(A,b)
    except:
       y=np.linalg.lstsq(A,b, rcond=None)[0]
    d=-(G.T @ y)
    d -= d.mean()
    # finite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)) or np.linalg.norm(d) < 1e-14:
       d = None
    if d is not None:
       pred = -G @ d
       # ensure d aligns with b? If some pred positive, red weighted could be low
       bb = float(np.dot(b,b))
       red = float(np.dot(b, pred)/bb)
       if not np.isfinite(red): red=0.0
       return d, red
    # fallback: use active sum gradient g = 2*conv? Build row sum
    # But can get gradient from G rows sum: grad = 2 * sum_r? Actually G rows contain derivatives; sum rows weights ones: grad = G.sum(axis=0) /? If k_act>0 and b ones, gradient of sum active C = sum_r row_r = G.sum(axis=0)
    grad = G.sum(axis=0)
    grad -= grad.mean()
    if np.linalg.norm(grad) > 1e-14:
       # scale to unit rms?
       rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad))
       d = grad / rms  # negative direction? Grad points increase; direction = -grad/rms
       d = -d
       red = 0.0 # unknown
       return d, red
    # odd fallback
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    d=i - (n-1)/2.0
    d -= d.mean()
    rms=np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d))
    if rms>0: d/=rms
    return d, 0.0
```

`active_indices` code:
```
def active_indices(c,M,K,max_active,active_frac=0.01):
    L=len(c)
    K=min(int(K),L)
    max_active=min(int(max_active),L)
    threshold = M - max(1e-12, active_frac*M)
    near = np.flatnonzero(c >= threshold)
    if near.size >= max_active:
        # top max_active among near
        part = np.argpartition(c[near], -max_active)[-max_active:]
        idx = near[part]
    else:
        if K < L:
            top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        else:
            top = np.arange(L)
        idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, near))) if near.size else top
        if idx.size > max_active:
            part = np.argpartition(c[idx], -max_active)[-max_active:]
            idx = idx[part]
    idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
    return idx
```
If near includes all top but `K` not include some near? We combine top K and near; if near<max_active but top K+near maybe >max_active? K≤max_active, near<max_active, sum could >max_active; cap.

`perturb_sequence` modes:
```
def perturb_sequence(a,sigma,rng,mode=0):
  n=len(a); S=a.sum()
  if mode==0 or rng.random()<0.7:
     b=np.maximum(a*np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)),0)
  else:
     # multiplicative sinusoidal
     f=1+0.3*np.sin(2*np.pi*(rng.uniform()*n+n?)+rng.uniform()*2π)
     b=np.maximum(a*f,0)
  s=b.sum(); if s<=0: b=a.copy(); else b*=S/s
  return b
```
No closures.

```
def optimize_lbfgs_from_a(a_in,T,budget,maxfun=1000):
    a=np.asarray(a_in,dtype=float); n=len(a); S=np.sqrt(2*n); a=normalize_to_S(a,S)
    theta=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)/a.sum())
    theta -= theta.mean()
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1,budget)
    state={'start':start,'deadline':deadline,'best_M':np.inf,'best_a':a.copy(),'last_obj':np.inf,'evals':0}
    # initial best
    c=fft_self(a); state['best_M']=c.max()
    try:
       res=minimize(lbfgs_func_grad, theta, args=(n,T,S,state), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':300,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-5})
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           # evaluate final
           a2=a_from_theta(res.x,S); M=fft_self(a2).max(); update if
    except Exception:
       pass
    return state['best_a'], state['best_M']
```

```
def normalize_to_S(a,S):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).copy()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 0:
      if a.size: a.fill(S/a.size)
   else:
      a *= S/s
   return a
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _nfft(size):
    return 1 << max(0, (size - 1).bit_length())
```
For size as int. If size=0? not.

`fft_self`: 
```
def fft_self(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    size=2*a.size-1
    n=_nfft(size)
    A=fft(a,n)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))
    return c[:size]
```
If a empty? not.

`fft_convolve_full` for L-BFGS:
```
def fft_convolve_full(a,b):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); b=np.asarray(b,float)
    out_len=len(a)+len(b)-1
    n=_nfft(out_len)
    return np.real(ifft(fft(a,n)*fft(b,n)))[:out_len]
```

`grad_from_weights`:
```
def grad_from_weights(w,a):
  n=len(a)
  ar=a[::-1]
  out_len=3*n-2
  full=fft_convolve_full(w, ar) # length out_len
  return 2.0 * full[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Check out_len: len(w)=2n-1 + len(ar)=n -> 3n-2. good.

```
def softmax_vec(theta):
   m=np.max(theta)
   e=np.exp(theta-m)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(len(theta),1/len)
   return e/s
```

`interpolate_to_length`:
```
def interpolate_to_length(a, n_out):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n_in=len(a)
   if n_out==n_in: return a.copy()
   if n_out<=1: return np.array([a[0]])
   if n_in<=1: return np.full(n_out,a[0])
   x_in=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_in)
   x_out=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_out)
   return np.interp(x_out,x_in,a)
```

`perturb_sequence`:
```
def perturb_sequence(a, sigma, rng, mode=0):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=len(a); S=a.sum()
   if S<=0: return normalize_to_S(np.ones(n)*? , S?) # but S target? Better pass? Here use current S_std? We'll keep it returns normalized to same sum.
   if mode==0:
      noise=np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
      b=a*noise
   elif mode==1:
      f=1.0 + 0.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*(np.arange(n)/n*(1.0+rng.random()*4.0)) + rng.uniform()*2*np.pi)
      b=a*f
   else:
      i=np.linspace(0,1,n)-0.5
      b=a*(1+sigma*i*4)
   b=np.maximum(b,0)
   s=b.sum()
   if s<=0: b=a.copy(); s=S
   return b*(a.sum()/s)
```
But when we call perturb, we want normalized to standard S, not necessarily current sum if current normalized S. `a.sum()` is S. Good. If a after perturb, normalize to S externally. Return normalized to same S.

Let's write raw_direction with no closures:
```
def raw_direction(a,c,M,n,K,max_active,active_frac=0.01):
    K=max(1,int(K)); max_active=max(K,int(max_active))
    idx=active_indices(c,M,K,max_active,active_frac)
    k=idx.size
    if k==0: return None,0
    G=np.empty((k,n),float)
    for r in range(k):
       row=G[r]
       np.fill(row,0.0)
       kk=int(idx[r])
       lo=max(0,kk-n+1); hi=min(n-1,kk)
       if lo<=hi:
          row[lo:hi+1] = 2.0 * a[kk-hi:kk-lo+1][::-1]
    b=np.ones(k)
    GGT=G@G.T
    diag=float(np.mean(np.diag(GGT))) if k else 0.0
    ridge=max(1e-10, diag*1e-7)
    A=GGT + ridge*np.eye(k)
    try:
        y=np.linalg.solve(A,b)
    except Exception:
        y=np.linalg.lstsq(A,b,rcond=None)[0]
    d=-(G.T @ y)
    d -= d.mean()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)): d=None
    if d is not None and np.linalg.norm(d) > 1e-14:
        pred=-G@d
        bb=float(np.dot(b,b))
        red=float(np.dot(b,pred)/bb)
        if not np.isfinite(red): red=0.0
        return d, red
    # fallback grad
    if k>0:
        g=G.sum(axis=0)
        g -= g.mean()
        rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g)))
        if rms > 1e-14:
            d=-g/rms
            pred=-G@d
            red=float(np.dot(b,pred)/float(np.dot(b,b)))
            if not np.isfinite(red): red=0.0
            return d, red
    i=np.arange(n,float)
    d=i - (n-1)/2.0
    d -= d.mean()
    rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)))
    if rms>0: d/=rms
    pred=-G@d
    red=float(np.dot(b,pred)/float(np.dot(b,b)))
    if not np.isfinite(red): red=0.0
    return d, red
```
In fallback grad, G may be large; if k large, G stored. okay.

`optimize_raw_active`:
```
def optimize_raw_active(a_in,budget,initial_K=24,max_K=80,target_frac=0.01,rng=None,state=None,tag='raw'):
   a=np.asarray(a_in,float)
   n=a.size
   if n==0 or budget<=0: return a_in, np.inf
   S=np.sqrt(2.0*float(n))
   a=normalize_to_S(a,S)
   local_best=a.copy(); local_best_score=np.inf
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+float(max(0.0,budget))
   c=fft_self(a); M=float(c.max()); local_best_score=M
   if state is not None: register_candidate(state,a,local_best_score,tag+'-init')
   K=initial_K; cur_frac=target_frac
   reject=0; no_improve=0; improves=0
   it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it += 1
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.2: break
       a=normalize_to_S(a,S)
       c=fft_self(a); M=float(c.max())
       if M < local_best_score - 1e-12:
           local_best_score=M; local_best=a.copy(); no_improve=0; improves+=1
           if state is not None: register_candidate(state,a,local_best_score,tag)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       # If current M decreased? compare previous? We don't store prev_M. Could not.
       if no_improve > 1500 and rng is not None:
           sigma=0.06 + 0.04*(it % 3)
           a=perturb_sequence(a, sigma, rng, mode=(it%2))
           a=normalize_to_S(a,S)
           no_improve=0; reject=0; cur_frac=max(0.002, target_frac)
           K=max(initial_K, K//2)
           continue
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.5: break
       d, red = raw_direction(a,c,M,n,K,max_K,active_frac=0.01)
       if d is None:
           if rng is not None:
              a=perturb_sequence(a,0.08,rng,mode=0); a=normalize_to_S(a,S); no_improve=0
           else:
              break
           continue
       target = max(1e-10, cur_frac * M)
       if red > 1e-8:
           base = target / red
       else:
           rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)))
           base = 0.02 if rms > 0.1 else 0.1
       base = float(min(max(base,1e-5), 0.5))
       # safe coeff for positivity
       max_coeff = 1.0
       neg = d < 0.0
       if np.any(neg):
           safe = np.min(np.maximum(a[neg], 1e-15) / (-d[neg]))
           max_coeff = min(0.95*safe, 1.0)
           if max_coeff < 1e-6:
               max_coeff = 1e-6
       cands=[]
       vals=(base, base*0.5, base*2.0, base*0.25, base*4.0, 0.01)
       for v in vals:
           v=float(min(max(v,1e-6), max_coeff, 1.0))
           if not any(abs(v-u)<1e-7 for u in cands):
               cands.append(v)
       best_cand=None; best_M_cand=M
       for coeff in cands:
           if coeff <=0: continue
           ac=a + coeff*d
           # remove tiny negatives
           if np.min(ac) < 0.0:
              ac[np.min(ac) < 0.0] = 0.0  # Wait np.min each? better if any.
```
Need write clip efficiently:

           if np.any(ac < 0.0):
               ac = np.maximum(ac, 0.0)
           s=ac.sum()
           if s<=1e-12: continue
           ac *= S/s
           Mc=float(fft_self(ac).max())
           if Mc < best_M_cand:
               best_M_cand=Mc; best_cand=ac
           if best_M_cand < M - 0.5*target:
               break
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.5: break
       if best_cand is not None:
           a=best_cand
           if best_M_cand < local_best_score - 1e-12:
              local_best_score=best_M_cand; local_best=a.copy(); no_improve=0; improves+=1; register
           else:
              # current improved but not local
              no_improve = max(0, no_improve-10)
           reject=0
           if cur_frac < target_frac:
              cur_frac = min(target_frac, cur_frac*2.0)
           if improves % 100 ==0:
              K = max(initial_K, K//2)
       else:
           reject +=1
           no_improve += 5
           if reject % 12 ==0:
              K = min(max_K, K + max(4, K//2))
              cur_frac = max(0.001, cur_frac*0.5)
           if reject > 40 and rng is not None:
              a=perturb_sequence(a,0.04,rng,mode=0); a=normalize_to_S(a,S); reject=0; cur_frac=max(0.002,cur_frac)
   return local_best, local_best_score
```

`lbfgs_func_grad(theta, n, T, S, state)`:
Need if `state['stop']` or time. After timeout, return zeros. But if first call not computed? We initialize state best and last_obj.
```
def lbfgs_func_grad(theta,n,T,S,state):
    if 'stop' in state and state['stop']:
       return state['last_obj'], np.zeros(len(theta))
    if time.monotonic() > state['deadline']:
       state['stop']=True
       return state['last_obj'], np.zeros(len(theta))
    a=a_from_theta(theta,S)
    c=fft_self(a)
    M=float(c.max())
    if M < state['best_M']:
       state['best_M']=M; state['best_a']=a.copy()
    scale=max(M,1e-12)
    if T <= 1.0:
       obj=M
       # weights one-hot top max? For gradient, choose all max indices
       w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[np.isclose(c,M,rtol=0,atol=1e-15)] = 1.0
       if w.sum()==0: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else:
       shifted=(T/scale)*(c-M)
       shifted=np.minimum(shifted,0.0)
       e=np.exp(shifted)
       s=e.sum()
       obj=M + (scale/T)*np.log(s)
       w=e/s
    grad_a=grad_from_weights(w,a)
    dot=float(np.dot(grad_a,a))
    grad_theta=grad_a*a - (a/S)*dot
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_theta)): grad_theta=np.zeros_like(theta)
    state['last_obj']=float(obj)
    state['evals']=state.get('evals',0)+1
    return float(obj), grad_theta
```
T may be e.g. 50. `T/scale` if scale normalized to M, okay. If scale very small, T/scale huge; but shifted max0; exponent underflow. Good.

```python
"""
Raw active-set Gauss-Newton search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The main optimizer works on sequences normalized to sum sqrt(2n), so the
objective is then just max(conv(a,a)).  For the current active lags it solves a
small least-norm active-set problem in primal variables, then performs a true
maximum line search.  This is supplemented by random perturbations, short
L-BFGS smooth-max passes, and multi-scale interpolation to larger lengths.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _nfft(size):
    size = int(size)
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << max(0, (size - 1).bit_length())


def fft_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _nfft(size)
    A = fft(a, nfft)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    return c[:size]


def fft_convolve_full(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = a.size + b.size - 1
    if out_len <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    nfft = _nfft(out_len)
    c = np.real(ifft(fft(a, nfft) * fft(b, nfft)))
    return c[:out_len]


def grad_from_weights(w, a):
    n = len(a)
    ar = a[::-1]
    out_len = 3 * n - 2
    full = fft_convolve_full(w, ar)
    return 2.0 * full[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def normalize_to_S(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a.fill(float(S) / float(a.size))
    else:
        a *= (float(S) / s)
    return a


def score_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    M = float(c.max())
    return float(2.0 * a.size * M / (s * s))


def register_candidate(state, a, score, tag="candidate"):
    if a is None:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    if score < state["best_score"] - 1e-12:
        state["best_score"] = score
        state["best_a"] = a.copy()
        now = time.monotonic()
        last = state.get("_last_print", 0.0)
        last_score = state.get("_last_print_score", np.inf)
        if last is None or (now - last > 25.0) or (score < last_score - 1e-4):
            print("[%s] n=%d score=%.7f" % (tag, a.size, score), flush=True)
            state["_last_print"] = now
            state["_last_print_score"] = score


def interpolate_to_length(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(m, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    x_in = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_out = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    return np.interp(x_out, x_in, a)


def perturb_sequence(a, sigma, rng, mode=0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy()
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return normalize_to_S(a, 1.0)

    mode = int(mode) % 3
    if mode == 0:
        b = a * np.exp(float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        out_freq = 1.0 + 5.0 * rng.random()
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        factor = 1.0 + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * out_freq * t + phase)
        factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.05)
        b = a * factor
    else:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n) - 0.5
        factor = 1.0 + 4.0 * float(sigma) * t
        factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.05)
        b = a * factor

    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    sb = float(b.sum())
    if sb <= 0.0:
        return a.copy()
    return b * (s / sb)


def active_indices(c, M, K, max_active, active_frac=0.01):
    L = c.size
    K = max(1, int(K))
    max_active = max(K, int(max_active))
    max_active = min(max_active, L)
    if K > L:
        K = L
    threshold = M - max(1e-12, float(active_frac) * M)
    near = np.flatnonzero(c >= threshold)
    if near.size >= max_active:
        sel = near[np.argpartition(c[near], -max_active)[-max_active:]]
    else:
        if K < L:
            top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        else:
            top = np.arange(L)
        if near.size:
            idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, near)))
        else:
            idx = top
        if idx.size > max_active:
            idx = idx[np.argpartition(c[idx], -max_active)[-max_active:]]
    idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
    return idx


def raw_direction(a, c, M, n, K, max_active, active_frac=0.01):
    idx = active_indices(c, M, K, max_active, active_frac)
    k = int(idx.size)
    if k <= 0:
        return None, 0.0

    G = np.empty((k, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(k):
        row = G[r]
        np.fill(row, 0.0)
        kk = int(idx[r])
        lo = max(0, kk - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, kk)
        if lo <= hi:
            row[lo:hi + 1] = 2.0 * a[kk - hi:kk - lo + 1][::-1]

    b = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64)
    GGT = G @ G.T
    diag = float(np.mean(np.diag(GGT))) if k else 0.0
    ridge = max(1e-10, 1e-7 * diag)
    A = GGT + ridge * np.eye(k, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        y = np.linalg.solve(A, b)
    except Exception:
        y = np.linalg.lstsq(A, b, rcond=None)[0]

    d = -(G.T @ y)
    d = d.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    d -= d.mean()

    if np.all(np.isfinite(d)) and float(np.linalg.norm(d)) > 1e-14:
        pred = -G @ d
        bb = float(np.dot(b, b))
        red = float(np.dot(b, pred) / bb) if bb > 0 else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(red):
            red = 0.0
        return d, red

    # Fallback: sum-gradient in primal variables.
    g = G.sum(axis=0)
    g = g - g.mean()
    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
    if np.all(np.isfinite(g)) and rms > 1e-14:
        d = -g / rms
        pred = -G @ d
        bb = float(np.dot(b, b))
        red = float(np.dot(b, pred) / bb) if bb > 0 else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(red):
            red = 0.0
        return d, red

    # Last-ditch odd perturbation about the center.
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d = i - 0.5 * (n - 1)
    d -= d.mean()
    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
    if rms > 0.0:
        d = d / rms
    else:
        d[:] = 0.0
    pred = -G @ d
    bb = float(np.dot(b, b))
    red = float(np.dot(b, pred) / bb) if bb > 0 else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(red):
        red = 0.0
    return d, red


def optimize_raw_active(a_in, budget, initial_K=24, max_K=80,
                        target_frac=0.01, rng=None, state=None, tag="raw"):
    a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if a.size == 0 or budget is None:
        return a, np.inf
    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 0.0:
        return a, np.inf

    n = int(a.size)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = normalize_to_S(a, S)

    local_best = a.copy()
    c0 = fft_self(a)
    local_best_score = float(c0.max())
    if state is not None:
        register_candidate(state, local_best, local_best_score, tag + "-init")

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + budget
    it = 0
    K = max(4, int(initial_K))
    max_K = max(K, int(max_K))
    cur_frac = max(0.001, float(target_frac))
    no_improve = 0
    reject = 0
    improves = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if (deadline - time.monotonic()) <= 0.3:
            break

        a = normalize_to_S(a, S)
        c = fft_self(a)
        M = float(c.max())

        if M < local_best_score - 1e-12:
            local_best_score = M
            local_best = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            improves += 1
            if state is not None:
                register_candidate(state, local_best, local_best_score, tag)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 1200 and rng is not None:
            sigma = 0.05 + 0.05 * float(it % 3)
            a = perturb_sequence(a, sigma, rng, mode=it % 3)
            a = normalize_to_S(a, S)
            no_improve = 0
            reject = 0
            cur_frac = max(0.002, float(target_frac))
            K = max(4, int(initial_K))
            continue

        if (deadline - time.monotonic()) <= 0.8:
            break

        d, red = raw_direction(a, c, M, n, K, max_K, active_frac=0.01)
        if d is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
            if rng is not None:
                a = perturb_sequence(a, 0.08, rng, mode=0)
                a = normalize_to_S(a, S)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                break
            continue

        target = max(1e-10, cur_frac * M)

        if red > 1e-8:
            base = target / red
        else:
            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
            base = 0.02 if rms > 0.1 else 0.10
        base = float(min(max(base, 1e-5), 0.5))

        max_coeff = 1.0
        neg = d < 0.0
        if np.any(neg):
            safe = np.min(np.maximum(a[neg], 1e-15) / (-d[neg]))
            max_coeff = min(0.95 * float(safe), 1.0)
            if max_coeff < 1e-6:
                max_coeff = 1e-6

        cands = []
        raw_vals = (base, 0.5 * base, 2.0 * base, 0.25 * base, 4.0 * base, 0.01)
        for v in raw_vals:
            v = float(min(max(v, 1e-6), max(max_coeff, 1e-6), 1.0))
            ok = True
            for u in cands:
                if abs(v - u) < 1e-7:
                    ok = False
                    break
            if ok:
                cands.append(v)

        best_cand = None
        best_M_cand = M
        for coeff in cands:
            if (deadline - time.monotonic()) <= 0.5:
                break
            ac = a + coeff * d
            if np.any(ac < 0.0):
                ac = np.maximum(ac, 0.0)
            s = float(ac.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                continue
            ac = ac * (S / s)
            Mc = float(fft_self(ac).max())
            if Mc < best_M_cand:
                best_M_cand = Mc
                best_cand = ac
            if best_M_cand < M - 0.5 * target:
                break

        if best_cand is not None:
            a = best_cand
            if best_M_cand < local_best_score - 1e-12:
                local_best_score = best_M_cand
                local_best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                improves += 1
                if state is not None:
                    register_candidate(state, local_best, local_best_score, tag)
            else:
                no_improve = max(0, no_improve - 10)
            reject = 0
            if cur_frac < float(target_frac):
                cur_frac = min(float(target_frac), 2.0 * cur_frac)
            if improves % 200 == 0:
                K = max(4, K // 2)
        else:
            reject += 1
            no_improve += 5
            if reject % 12 == 0:
                K = min(max_K, K + max(4, K // 2))
                cur_frac = max(0.001, 0.5 * cur_frac)
            if reject > 40 and rng is not None:
                a = perturb_sequence(a, 0.04, rng, mode=0)
                a = normalize_to_S(a, S)
                reject = 0
                cur_frac = max(0.002, cur_frac)

    return local_best, local_best_score


def softmax_vec(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(theta))
    e = np.exp(theta - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(theta.size, 1.0 / theta.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def a_from_theta(theta, S):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = theta.size
    p = softmax_vec(theta)
    return float(S) * p


def theta_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.log(a / s)
    theta -= float(np.mean(theta))
    return theta


def lbfgs_func_grad(theta, n, T, S, state):
    if state.get("_stop", False):
        return float(state.get("_last_obj", np.inf)), np.zeros(len(theta), dtype=np.float64)

    if time.monotonic() > state["_deadline"]:
        state["_stop"] = True
        return float(state.get("_last_obj", np.inf)), np.zeros(len(theta), dtype=np.float64)

    a = a_from_theta(theta, S)
    c = fft_self(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M < state["_best_M"]:
        state["_best_M"] = M
        state["_best_a"] = a.copy()

    T = float(T)
    scale = max(M, 1e-12)

    if T <= 1.0:
        obj = M
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        if c.size:
            mask = c >= (M - 1e-12)
            if not np.any(mask):
                mask = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=bool)
                mask[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
            w[mask] = 1.0 / float(mask.sum())
    else:
        shifted = (T / scale) * (c - M)
        shifted = np.minimum(shifted, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(shifted)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if se <= 0.0:
            se = 1.0
            e = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            e[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        obj = M + (scale / T) * np.log(se)
        w = e / se

    grad_a = grad_from_weights(w, a)
    dot = float(np.dot(grad_a, a))
    grad_theta = grad_a * a - (a / float(S)) * dot
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_theta)):
        grad_theta = np.zeros(len(theta), dtype=np.float64)

    state["_last_obj"] = float(obj)
    state["_evals"] = int(state.get("_evals", 0)) + 1
    return float(obj), grad_theta


def optimize_lbfgs_from_a(a_in, T, budget, tag="lbfgs"):
    a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if a.size == 0 or budget is None:
        return a, np.inf
    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 0.0:
        return a, np.inf

    n = int(a.size)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = normalize_to_S(a, S)
    theta = theta_from_a(a)

    start = time.monotonic()
    state = {
        "_start": start,
        "_deadline": start + budget,
        "_stop": False,
        "_best_M": np.inf,
        "_best_a": a.copy(),
        "_last_obj": np.inf,
        "_evals": 0,
    }

    c0 = fft_self(a)
    state["_best_M"] = float(c0.max())

    maxfun = int(max(200, min(3000, budget / 0.02)))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lbfgs_func_grad,
            theta,
            args=(n, float(T), float(S), state),
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": 300,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "maxls": 20,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-5,
                "maxcor": 20,
            },
        )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            a2 = a_from_theta(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), S)
            a2 = normalize_to_S(a2, S)
            M2 = float(fft_self(a2).max())
            if np.isfinite(M2) and M2 < state["_best_M"]:
                state["_best_M"] = M2
                state["_best_a"] = a2
    except Exception:
        pass

    return state["_best_a"], float(state["_best_M"])


def make_initial_sequence(n, rng):
    n = max(16, int(n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = (1.0 + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t) + 0.25 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * t)
         + 0.12 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.01)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    return normalize_to_S(a, S)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    total_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 45.0)
    deadline = total_start + total_budget

    state = {
        "best_a": None,
        "best_score": np.inf,
        "_last_print": 0.0,
        "_last_print_score": np.inf,
    }

    parent_a = None
    parent_score = np.inf

    try:
        raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        raw = np.where(np.isfinite(raw), raw, 0.0)
        raw = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        raw = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if raw.size >= 8 and float(raw.sum()) > 0.01:
        n = int(raw.size)
        a = normalize_to_S(raw, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)))
        sc = score_fft(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            parent_a = a.copy()
            parent_score = sc
            state["best_a"] = a.copy()
            state["best_score"] = sc
            print("loaded parent n=%d internal_score=%.7f" % (n, sc), flush=True)
    else:
        n0 = 4096
        a = make_initial_sequence(n0, rng)
        sc = score_fft(a)
        state["best_a"] = a.copy()
        state["best_score"] = sc
        print("no valid parent; generated n=%d internal_score=%.7f" % (n0, sc), flush=True)

    # Main polish from the inherited construction.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    b1 = min(0.44 * total_budget, remaining - 12.0)
    if b1 > 20.0 and state["best_a"] is not None:
        _, _ = optimize_raw_active(
            state["best_a"], b1,
            initial_K=24, max_K=88, target_frac=0.010,
            rng=rng, state=state, tag="raw-main"
        )

    # Short smooth L-BFGS passes.
    for T in (80.0, 220.0):
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        b = min(0.055 * total_budget, remaining - 25.0)
        if b < 20.0 or state["best_a"] is None:
            break
        a, s = optimize_lbfgs_from_a(state["best_a"], T, b, tag="lbfgs-T%d" % int(T))
        if a is not None and np.isfinite(s):
            register_candidate(state, a, s, "lbfgs-T%d" % int(T))

    # Second raw pass.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    b2 = min(0.12 * total_budget, remaining - 80.0)
    if b2 > 20.0 and state["best_a"] is not None:
        _, _ = optimize_raw_active(
            state["best_a"], b2,
            initial_K=28, max_K=96, target_frac=0.008,
            rng=rng, state=state, tag="raw-second"
        )

    # Multi-scale: interpolate to a larger length, then polish.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 220.0 and state["best_a"] is not None:
        target_len = 8192
        if state["best_a"].size != target_len:
            a8 = interpolate_to_length(state["best_a"], target_len)
        else:
            a8 = state["best_a"].copy()
        a8 = normalize_to_S(a8, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(target_len)))
        b8 = min(0.16 * total_budget, remaining - 140.0)
        if b8 > 40.0:
            _, _ = optimize_raw_active(
                a8, b8,
                initial_K=36, max_K=104, target_frac=0.008,
                rng=rng, state=state, tag="len8192"
            )

    # Very large length only if it looks beneficial and time is ample.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 420.0 and state["best_a"] is not None and state["best_score"] > 1.5005:
        target_len = 16384
        if state["best_a"].size != target_len:
            a16 = interpolate_to_length(state["best_a"], target_len)
        else:
            a16 = state["best_a"].copy()
        a16 = normalize_to_S(a16, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(target_len)))
        b16 = min(0.09 * total_budget, remaining - 170.0)
        if b16 > 50.0:
            _, _ = optimize_raw_active(
                a16, b16,
                initial_K=44, max_K=120, target_frac=0.007,
                rng=rng, state=state, tag="len16384"
            )

    # A few local escape attempts from the best point.
    for mode in (0, 2):
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        b = min(0.035 * total_budget, remaining - 60.0)
        if b < 25.0 or state["best_a"] is None:
            break
        ap = perturb_sequence(state["best_a"], 0.18, rng, mode=mode)
        ap = normalize_to_S(ap, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(ap.size)))
        _, _ = optimize_raw_active(
            ap, b,
            initial_K=28, max_K=96, target_frac=0.010,
            rng=rng, state=state, tag="escape-%d" % mode
        )

    # Final selection among sanitized internal candidates.
    if state["best_a"] is None:
        a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        a = normalize_to_S(a, np.sqrt(2.0 * 256.0))
        state["best_a"] = a
        state["best_score"] = score_fft(a)

    best = np.asarray(state["best_a"], dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    best = np.where(np.isfinite(best), best, 0.0)
    best = np.clip(best, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if best.size == 0 or best.sum() <= 0.01:
        best = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    candidates = [best]
    if parent_a is not None:
        candidates.append(parent_a)
    if best.size > 1:
        candidates.append(best[::-1].copy())

    chosen = best
    chosen_score = float("inf")
    for cand in candidates:
        cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if cand.size == 0 or cand.sum() <= 0.01:
            continue
        sc = score_fft(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < chosen_score:
            chosen_score = sc
            chosen = cand

    chosen = normalize_to_S(chosen, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(chosen.size)))
    chosen_list = [float(x) for x in chosen.tolist()]

    print("final internal score=%.7f n=%d" % (chosen_score, len(chosen_list)), flush=True)

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(chosen_list))
        if not np.isfinite(verified):
            raise ValueError("non-finite verified score")
        print("evaluate_sequence final=%.7f" % verified, flush=True)
    except Exception:
        fallback = [1.0] * 256
        try:
            print("fallback evaluate_sequence=%.7f" % float(evaluate_sequence(fallback)), flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass
        chosen_list = fallback

    return chosen_list
```
